normalizeWorkspacePath,\n  readWorkspaceFile,\n  requireScanRoots,\n  resolveInsideWorkspace as resolveAdapterPath,\n  workspacePathExists,\n} from './adapter-fs';\n\ntype FindingRule =\n  | 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test'\n  | 'migration_without_test'\n  | 'security_source_without_security_test';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly allowlist?: readonly string[];\n  readonly includeWriteBoundaryFindings?: boolean;\n  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[]; readonly snapshot_hash?: string; readonly repo_state_id?: string };\n}\n\ninterface EvidenceRef {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterObservation {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly type: string;\n  readonly path?: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly name?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: FindingRule;\n  readonly severity: 'medium' | 'high';\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly evidence: readonly EvidenceRef[];\n  readonly confidence?: number;\n  readonly actionability?: 'actionable' | 'review_required';\n  readonly review_reason?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface MemoryCandidate {\n  readonly belief_id: string;\n  readonly claim: string;\n  readonly confidence: number;\n  readonly evidence_refs: readonly string[];\n  readonly source_tool_id: string;\n}\n\ninterface AriaOutput {\n  readonly observations: readonly AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: readonly AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly read_paths: readonly string[];\n  readonly evidence_sources: readonly string[];\n  readonly belief_candidates: readonly MemoryCandidate[];\n  readonly cost_units: number;\n  readonly metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface FileUnit {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly relativePath: string;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly sourceFile: ts.SourceFile;\n  readonly isTest: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SourceRisk {\n  readonly riskClass: string;\n  readonly highRisk: boolean;\n  readonly securitySensitive: boolean;\n  readonly migrationHazard: boolean;\n  readonly writeBoundary: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface AnalysisResult {\n  readonly observations: AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly readPaths: string[];\n}\n\ninterface PathAlias {\n  readonly prefix: string;\n  readonly suffix: string;\n  readonly targets: readonly string[];\n}\n\n\nexport function analyzeTestGaps(input: AdapterInput, workspaceRoot = process.cwd()): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('test-gap-adapter', input.roots);\n  const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);","numLines":140,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uf8FowjYDzadYc5s4M4Nud","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Auth Service Health Controller Unit Tests\n3\t *\n4\t * Tests the standardized health check format:\n5\t *   GET /health/live  -> { status: 'ok' }\n6\t *   GET /health/ready -> { status, checks: { database } }\n7\t *   GET /health       -> { status, timestamp, uptime, version }\n8\t */\n9\t\n10\timport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\n11\timport { DataSource } from 'typeorm';\n12\t\n13\timport { HealthController } from '../health.controller';\n14\t\n15\t// Mock response object for @Res() endpoints\n16\tconst createMockResponse = () => {\n17\t  const res: any = {\n18\t    status: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n19\t    json: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n20\t  };\n21\t  return res;\n22\t};\n23\t\n24\tconst restoreEnv = (key: string, value: string | undefined) => {\n25\t  if (value === undefined) {\n26\t    delete process.env[key];\n27\t  } else {\n28\t    process.env[key] = value;\n29\t  }\n30\t};\n31\t\n32\tdescribe('HealthController (Auth Service)', () => {\n33\t  let controller: HealthController;\n34\t  let queryMock: jest.Mock;\n35\t  let isInitialized: boolean;\n36\t  const originalNodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;\n37\t  const originalAquaEnv = process.env.AQUA_ENV;\n38\t  const originalDeployEnv = process.env.DEPLOY_ENV;\n39\t  const originalMfaKey = process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY;\n40\t\n41\t  const createMockDataSource = () => ({\n42\t    get isInitialized() {\n43\t      return isInitialized;\n44\t    },\n45\t    query: queryMock,\n46\t  });\n47\t\n48\t  beforeEach(async () => {\n49\t    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test';\n50\t    delete process.env.AQUA_ENV;\n51\t    delete process.env.DEPLOY_ENV;\n52\t    delete process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY;\n53\t    isInitialized = true;\n54\t    queryMock = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ '?column?': 1 }]);\n55\t\n56\t    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n57\t      controllers: [HealthController],\n58\t      providers: [\n59\t        {\n60\t          provide: DataSource,\n61\t          useFactory: createMockDataSource,\n62\t        },\n63\t      ],\n64\t    }).compile();\n65\t\n66\t    controller = module.get<HealthController>(HealthController);\n67\t  });\n68\t\n69\t  afterEach(() => {\n70\t    restoreEnv('NODE_ENV', originalNodeEnv);\n71\t    restoreEnv('AQUA_ENV', originalAquaEnv);\n72\t    restoreEnv('DEPLOY_ENV', originalDeployEnv);\n73\t    restoreEnv('MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY', originalMfaKey);\n74\t  });\n75\t\n76\t  describe('liveness', () => {\n77\t    it('should return ok status', () => {\n78\t      const result = controller.liveness();\n79\t      expect(result).toEqual({ status: 'ok' });\n80\t    });\n81\t\n82\t    it('should always succeed regardless of database state', () => {\n83\t      isInitialized = false;\n84\t      const result = controller.liveness();\n85\t      expect(result).toEqual({ status: 'ok' });\n86\t    });\n87\t  });\n88\t\n89\t  describe('readiness', () => {\n90\t    it('should return ok when database is connected', async () => {\n91\t      isInitialized = true;\n92\t      queryMock.mockResolvedValue([{ '?column?': 1 }]);\n93\t      const res = createMockResponse();\n94\t\n95\t      await controller.readiness(res);\n96\t\n97\t      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(200);\n98\t      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n99\t        status: 'ok',\n100\t        checks: { database: 'ok' },\n101\t      });\n102\t    });\n103\t\n104\t    it('should return 503 when database is not connected', async () => {\n105\t      isInitialized = false;\n106\t      const res = * Auth Service Health Controller Unit Tests\n *\n * Tests the standardized health check format:\n *   GET /health/live  -> { status: 'ok' }\n *   GET /health/ready -> { status, checks: { database } }\n *   GET /health       -> { status, timestamp, uptime, version }\n */\n\nimport { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';\nimport { DataSource } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport { HealthController } from '../health.controller';\n\n// Mock response object for @Res() endpoints\nconst createMockResponse = () => {\n  const res: any = {\n    status: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n    json: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),\n  };\n  return res;\n};\n\nconst restoreEnv = (key: string, value: string | undefined) => {\n  if (value === undefined) {\n    delete process.env[key];\n  } else {\n    process.env[key] = value;\n  }\n};\n\ndescribe('HealthController (Auth Service)', () => {\n  let controller: HealthController;\n  let queryMock: jest.Mock;\n  let isInitialized: boolean;\n  const originalNodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV;\n  const originalAquaEnv = process.env.AQUA_ENV;\n  const originalDeployEnv = process.env.DEPLOY_ENV;\n  const originalMfaKey = process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY;\n\n  const createMockDataSource = () => ({\n    get isInitialized() {\n      return isInitialized;\n    },\n    query: queryMock,\n  });\n\n  beforeEach(async () => {\n    process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test';\n    delete process.env.AQUA_ENV;\n    delete process.env.DEPLOY_ENV;\n    delete process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY;\n    isInitialized = true;\n    queryMock = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ '?column?': 1 }]);\n\n    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({\n      controllers: [HealthController],\n      providers: [\n        {\n          provide: DataSource,\n          useFactory: createMockDataSource,\n        },\n      ],\n    }).compile();\n\n    controller = module.get<HealthController>(HealthController);\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    restoreEnv('NODE_ENV', originalNodeEnv);\n    restoreEnv('AQUA_ENV', originalAquaEnv);\n    restoreEnv('DEPLOY_ENV', originalDeployEnv);\n    restoreEnv('MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY', originalMfaKey);\n  });\n\n  describe('liveness', () => {\n    it('should return ok status', () => {\n      const result = controller.liveness();\n      expect(result).toEqual({ status: 'ok' });\n    });\n\n    it('should always succeed regardless of database state', () => {\n      isInitialized = false;\n      const result = controller.liveness();\n      expect(result).toEqual({ status: 'ok' });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('readiness', () => {\n    it('should return ok when database is connected', async () => {\n      isInitialized = true;\n      queryMock.mockResolvedValue([{ '?column?': 1 }]);\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(200);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'ok',\n        checks: { database: 'ok' },\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return 503 when database is not connected', async () => {\n      isInitialized = false;\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(503);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'not_ready',\n        checks: { database: 'error' },\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return 503 when database query fails', async () => {\n      isInitialized = true;\n      queryMock.mockRejectedValue(new Error('Connection refused'));\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(503);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'not_ready',\n        checks: { database: 'error' },\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return 503 when production MFA encryption key is missing', async () => {\n      process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';\n      delete process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY;\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(503);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'not_ready',\n        checks: { database: 'ok', mfaEncryptionKey: 'error' },\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return 503 when production MFA encryption key is malformed', async () => {\n      process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';\n      process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY = 'not-a-hex-key';\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(503);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'not_ready',\n        checks: { database: 'ok', mfaEncryptionKey: 'error' },\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('should return ok when production MFA encryption key is valid', async () => {\n      process.env.NODE_ENV = 'production';\n      process.env.MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY = 'a'.repeat(64);\n      const res = createMockResponse();\n\n      await controller.readiness(res);\n\n      expect(res.status).toHaveBeenCalledWith(200);\n      expect(res.json).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n        status: 'ok',\n        checks: { database: 'ok', mfaEncryptionKey: 'ok' },\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('health', () => {\n    it('should return standardized health status', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n\n      expect(result.status).toBe('ok');\n      expect(result.timestamp).toBeDefined();\n      expect(typeof result.uptime).toBe('number');\n      expect(result.uptime).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n      expect(result.version).toBeDefined();\n    });\n\n    it('should include valid ISO timestamp', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n      const timestamp = new Date(result.timestamp);\n      expect(timestamp.toISOString()).toBe(result.timestamp);\n    });\n\n    it('should not expose database status in public health endpoint', () => {\n      const result = controller.health() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;\n      expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('database');\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":197,"startLine":1,"totalLines":197}}}

expect(queryMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith('SELECT 1');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('GET /health (general health)', () => {\n    it('should return standardized health response', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n\n      expect(result.status).toBe('ok');\n      expect(result.timestamp).toBeDefined();\n      expect(typeof result.uptime).toBe('number');\n      expect(result.uptime).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n      expect(result.version).toBeDefined();\n    });\n\n    it('should include valid ISO timestamp', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n      const timestamp = new Date(result.timestamp);\n      expect(timestamp.toISOString()).toBe(result.timestamp);\n    });\n\n    it('should report actual process uptime', () => {\n      const beforeUptime = process.uptime();\n      const result = controller.health();\n      const afterUptime = process.uptime();\n\n      expect(result.uptime).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(beforeUptime);\n      expect(result.uptime).toBeLessThanOrEqual(afterUptime);\n    });\n\n    it('should not expose sensitive data', () => {\n      const result = controller.health() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;\n      expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('database');\n      expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('memory');\n      expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('connections');\n    });\n\n    it('should include service name (ADR-013 Section 8.4)', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n      expect(result.service).toBeDefined();\n      expect(typeof result.service).toBe('string');\n    });\n\n    it('should include framework version info for NestJS v10/v11 identification (ADR-013 Section 8.4)', () => {\n      const result = controller.health();\n\n      expect(result.framework).toBeDefined();\n      expect(typeof result.framework.nestjs).toBe('string');\n      expect(typeof result.framework.express).toBe('string');\n      expect(typeof result.framework.node).toBe('string');\n\n      // NestJS and Express versions should be semver-like or 'unknown'\n      expect(result.framework.nestjs).toMatch(/^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+|unknown$/);\n      expect(result.framework.express).toMatch(/^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+|unknown$/);\n\n      // Node version starts with 'v' (e.g. 'v20.11.0')\n      expect(result.framework.node).toMatch(/^v\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+/);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('extensibility via getAdditionalChecks()', () => {\n    @Controller('health')\n    class ExtendedHealthController extends StandardHealthController {\n      constructor(dataSource: DataSource) {\n        super(dataSource);\n        this.serviceName = 'test-service';\n      }\n\n      protected override async getAdditionalChecks(): Promise<Record<string, 'ok' | 'error'>> {\n        return { custom: 'ok' };\n      }\n    }\n\n    let extController: ExtendedHealthController;\n\n (reflector: Reflector, configService: ConfigService): TenantGuard =>\n359\t        new TenantGuard(reflector, undefined, configService),\n360\t      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService],\n361\t    },\n362\t    {\n363\t      provide: APP_GUARD,\n364\t      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector): RolesGuard => new RolesGuard(reflector),\n365\t      inject: [Reflector],\n366\t    },\n367\t    {\n368\t      provide: APP_GUARD,\n369\t      useFactory: (\n370\t        reflector: Reflector,\n371\t        configService: ConfigService,\n372\t        rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy,\n373\t      ): ThrottlerGuard => new ThrottlerGuard(reflector, configService, rateLimiter),\n374\t      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService, SlidingWindowStrategy],\n375\t    },\n376\t    /** SEC-M22: Register global audit logging for compliance — all mutations are tracked. */\n377\t    {\n378\t      provide: APP_INTERCEPTOR,\n379\t      useClass: AuditLogInterceptor,\n380\t    },\n381\t    // Bootstrap source ],\n388\t})\n389\texport class AppModule implements NestModule {\n390\t  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer) {\n391\t    // Middleware execution order:\n392\t    // 1. TenantContextMiddleware - Extract tenant from JWT/headers\n395\t    // 4. TenantSchemaMiddleware - Set PostgreSQL search_path to tenant schema\n396\t    // SEC-CRITICAL-002 sweep — strip forged internal headers (safe on all routes\n397\t    // except the health probe).\n398\t    consumer\n399\t      .apply(StripInternalHeadersMiddleware)\n400\t      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n401\t      .forRoutes('*');\n402\t\n403\t    // SEC-HIGH-156: resolve req.user/req.tenantId from the gateway-signed\n404\t    // verified-user assertion (after Strip sets req.verifiedIdentity, before\n405\t    // UserContext). ai-service serves no direct browser REST surface — the\n406\t    // former /api/v2/ai proxy was retired for NATS request.ai.chat — so only\n407\t    // the health probe is excluded (tests/invariants/nginx-route-resolution.spec.ts\n408\t    // keeps nginx and the route table in agreement).\n409\t    consumer\n410\t      .apply(VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware)\n411\t      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n412\t logging\n418\t        UserContextMiddleware,\n419\t        TenantContextMiddleware,\n420\t        TenantSchemaMiddleware,\n421\t      )\n422\t      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n423\t      .forRoutes('*');\n424\t { createHash } from 'crypto';\nimport { join } from 'path';\nimport { Module, NestModule, MiddlewareConsumer } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\nimport { GraphQLModule } from '@nestjs/graphql';\nimport { APP_GUARD, APP_INTERCEPTOR, Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';\nimport { ApolloFederationDriver, ApolloFederationDriverConfig } from '@nestjs/apollo';\nimport { DocumentNode, GraphQLError, GraphQLSchema } from 'graphql';\nimport depthLimit from 'graphql-depth-limit';\nimport { fieldExtensionsEstimator, getComplexity, simpleEstimator } from 'graphql-query-complexity';\nimport { PlatformJwtModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/auth';\nimport {\n  AuditLogModule,\n  AuditLogInterceptor,\n  AuditedOperationModule,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/audit';\nimport { TenantErasureTargetModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/compliance';\nimport {\n  AuditColumnsModule,\n  createSchemaVersionGate,\n  createServiceTypeOrmConfig,\n  createTenantConnectionBootstrap,\n  isSchemaDdlOwnedByDbMigrate,\n  RlsModule,\n  getRlsExcludeTablesForService,\n  SchemaDriftModule,\n  SourceSchemaBootstrapService,\n  TenantSchemaSyncService,\n  TenantSchemaCacheModule,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\nimport { TenantExecutionContextModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/context';\nimport { RolesGuard, TenantGuard } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/guards';\nimport { RequestContextMiddleware } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/logging';\nimport { ServiceMetricsModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/metrics';\nimport {\n  CorrelationIdMiddleware,\n  createTenantSchemaMiddleware,\n  StripInternalHeadersMiddleware,\n  VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware,\n  TenantContextMiddleware,\n  UserContextMiddleware,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/middleware';\nimport { RedisModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/redis';\nimport { CircuitBreakerModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/resilience';\nimport {\n  SlidingWindowStrategy,\n  ThrottlerGuard,\n  ThrottlerModule,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/security';\nconst TenantSchemaMiddleware = createTenantSchemaMiddleware('ai');\nconst TenantConnectionBootstrap = createTenantConnectionBootstrap('ai');\n\n/**\n * AiMigrationRunnerService — schema-version gate for the ai source schema\n * (Faz 1.5 of the day-one baseline reset).\n *\n * Was `createMigrationRunnerService('ai')`. Now uses\n * `createSchemaVersionGate('ai')`:\n *\n *   • production (`DB_MIGRATE_AUTHORITATIVE=true`) — read-only ledger\n *     probe; refuses boot if aqua-db-migrate has not finalised `ai`.\n *   • development (default)                       — delegates to the\n *     runner verbatim, preserving dev/test ergonomics.\n *\n * migrations/ starts empty — ai-service currently relies on\n * SourceSchemaBootstrapService + TenantSchemaSyncService. The gate is\n * wired so future migrations land deterministically through\n * aqua-db-migrate.\n */\nconst AiMigrationRunnerService = createSchemaVersionGate('ai');\nconst aiSchemaDdlOwnedByDbMigrate = isSchemaDdlOwnedByDbMigrate(process.env);\nimport { EventBusModule, buildEventBusConfig } from '@platform/event-bus';\nimport { HealthModule } from './health/health.module';\nimport { ToolRegistryModule } from './tools/tool-registry.module';\nimport { WaterChemistryToolsModule } from './tools/water-chemistry/water-chemistry-tools.module';\nimport { SensorConfigToolsModule } from './tools/sensor-config/sensor-config-tools.module';\nimport { FarmToolsModule } from './tools/farm/farm-tools.module';\nimport { AquacultureMathToolsModule } from './tools/aquaculture-math/aquaculture-math-tools.module';\nimport { ConversationModule } from './conversation/conversation.module';\nimport { AgentConfigModule } from './tenant-config/agent-config.module';\nimport { AuditModule } from './audit/audit.module';\nimport { CostModule } from './cost/cost.module';\nimport { ChatModule } from './chat/chat.module';\nimport { SensorChannelDetectionModule } from './sensor-detection/sensor-channel-detection.module';\nimport { AiOutboxModule } from './outbox/ai-outbox.module';\n\n// Entities\nimport { AgentConversation } from './conversation/conversation.entity';\nimport { ActionsModule } from './actions/actions.module';\nimport { ProposedAction } from './actions/proposed-action.entity';\nimport { TenantAgentConfig } from './tenant-config/agent-config.entity';\nimport { ToolExecutionAudit } from './audit/tool-execution-audit.entity';\nimport { ConversationTurn } from './cost/conversation-turn.entity';\nimport { AiOutbox } from './outbox/ai-outbox.entity';\nimport { ScheduledJobModule } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/scheduling';\n\n// Per-process cache for GraphQL complexity results keyed by document hash.\n// This avoids recomputing complexity for identical operations on every request.\nconst complexityCache = new Map<string, number>();\n/** SEC-LOW-091: LRU-ish ceiling for the complexity cache (insert-order eviction). */\nconst COMPLEXITY_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 1000;\n\ntype QueryComplexityOperationContext = {\n  request: {\n    query?: string;\n    operationName?: string;\n    variables?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  };\n  document: DocumentNode;\n  schema: GraphQLSchema;\n};\n\n@Module({\n  imports: [\n    ConfigModule.forRoot({\n      isGlobal: true,\n      envFilePath: ['.env.local', '.env'],\n    }),\n    // CIRCUIT-CRITICAL-001 cure: register the @Global canonical\n    // CircuitBreakerService so feature modules (AgentRunnerService at\n    // ai-service/src/agent/...) can constructor-inject it without\n    // per-module re-import. Wraps the Anthropic API call (and any\n    // future external IO) in a sliding-window breaker with fail-CLOSED\n    // semantics and per-tenant keying.\n    CircuitBreakerModule,\n    // Database connection — uses the platform TypeORM factory.\n    // INTENTIONAL: no `schema:` — TenantConnectionBootstrap manages\n    // search_path per request. AiMigrationRunnerService (provider above)\n    // executes migrations at OnApplicationBootstrap; factory's\n    // migrationsRun:false default keeps TypeORM out of that codepath.\n    // INFRA-DB-SSL-001 fix: DB_SSL → DATABASE_SSL via factory.\n    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({\n      imports: [ConfigModule],\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) =>\n        createServiceTypeOrmConfig(configService, {\n          serviceName: 'ai',\n          schema: 'ai',\n          // FAZ0-BOOT-02: AiOutbox MUST be in this list — OutboxModule.forFeature\n          // only wires repositories/publishers; the DataSource learns entity\n          // metadata solely from here. Omitting it broke outbox repository DI\n          // and left the outbox table out of SourceSchemaBootstrap/TenantSchemaSync\n          // (messaging-service registers MessagingOutbox the same way).\n          entities: [\n            AgentConversation,\n            TenantAgentConfig,\n            ToolExecutionAudit,\n            ConversationTurn,\n            AiOutbox,\n            ProposedAction,\n          ],\n          migrations: [__dirname + '/database/migrations/[0-9]*.{js,ts}'],\n          // INFRA-CRITICAL-020 contract: env-aware migration timing.\n          // - Production: DATABASE_MIGRATIONS_RUN=false (default). The\n          //   aqua-db-migrate container runs migrations BEFORE service\n          //   containers start, so this service's TypeORM does NOT touch\n          //   the migration table at boot — MigrationRunnerService below\n          //   verifies the schema is healthy and proceeds.\n          // - E2E tests: harness sets DATABASE_MIGRATIONS_RUN=true so\n          //   TypeORM runs migrations at DataSource init — BEFORE the\n          //   SourceSchemaBootstrapService onApplicationBootstrap hook\n          //   fires, which would otherwise hard-fail on an empty source\n          //   schema (INFRA-CRITICAL-009, INFRA-CRITICAL-020).\n          migrationsRunFromEnv: (cs) =>\n            cs.get<string>('DATABASE_MIGRATIONS_RUN', 'false') === 'true',\n        }),\n    }),\n    GraphQLModule.forRootAsync<ApolloFederationDriverConfig>({\n      driver: ApolloFederationDriver,\n      imports: [ConfigModule],\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) => {\n        const isProduction = configService.get('NODE_ENV') === 'production';\n        return {\n          autoSchemaFile: {\n            // Emit the federated SDL to the registry-canonical artifact path so\n            // the supergraph build + validate-registry pick up the ai subgraph\n            // (must match gatewaySubgraph('ai').schemaArtifactPath in the catalog).\n            path: join(process.cwd(), 'dist/graphql/subgraphs/ai.graphql'),\n            federation: 2,\n          },\n          /** SEC-M21: Disable GraphQL query batching to prevent batch-based brute-force attacks.\n           *  The gateway already blocks batching, but subgraphs must also enforce this as\n           *  defense-in-depth in case a subgraph becomes directly accessible. */\n          allowBatchedHttpRequests: false,\n          /**\n           * Keep Apollo CSRF prevention explicit as defense in depth against\n           * cross-site search and simple-request execution paths.\n           */\n          csrfPrevention: true,\n          playground: false,\n          graphiql: process.env['NODE_ENV'] !== 'production',\n          validationRules: [depthLimit(10)],\n          plugins: [\n            {\n              requestDidStart: async () => ({\n                async didResolveOperation({\n                  request,\n                  document,\n                  schema,\n                }: QueryComplexityOperationContext) {\n                  // Cache complexity by document hash to avoid re-computation for\n                  // identical operations. The hash key incorporates the operation name\n                  // so distinct named operations in the same document are treated separately.\n                  const docSource = request.query ?? '';\n                  const opName = request.operationName ?? '';\n                  /** SEC-L01: Use SHA-256 instead of deprecated SHA-1 for cache key generation.\n                   *  SHA-1 has known collision vulnerabilities (SHAttered attack, 2017). */\n                  const cacheKey = createHash('sha256')\n                    .update(docSource)\n                    .update('\\x00')\n                    .update(opName)\n                    .digest('hex');\n\n                  // SEC-LOW-091 (2026-08-23 scan №36): bounded cache — unique\n                  // query strings used to grow the Map indefinitely.\n                  if (complexityCache.size >= COMPLEXITY_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) {\n                    const oldest = complexityCache.keys().next().value;\n                    if (oldest !== undefined) complexityCache.delete(oldest);\n                  }\n                  let complexity = complexityCache.get(cacheKey);\n                  if (complexity === undefined) {\n                    complexity = getComplexity({\n                      schema,\n                      operationName: request.operationName,\n                      query: document,\n                      variables: request.variables,\n                      estimators: [\n                        fieldExtensionsEstimator(),\n                        simpleEstimator({ defaultComplexity: 1 }),\n                      ],\n                    });\n                    complexityCache.set(cacheKey, complexity);\n                  }\n\n                  const maxComplexity = 1000;\n                  if (complexity > maxComplexity) {\n                    throw new GraphQLError(\n                      `Query too complex: ${complexity}. Maximum allowed: ${maxComplexity}`,\n                    );\n                  }\n                },\n              }),\n            },\n          ],\n          // 2026-04-30: Deprecated GraphQL Playground is not enabled at runtime.\n          // WHY: subgraphs must not depend on deprecated Apollo developer UI behavior.\n          /** SEC-NEW06: Disable introspection override — gateway handles introspection centrally.\n           *  Subgraph introspection in production exposes internal schema details. */\n          introspection: !isProduction,\n          context: ({ req }: { req: Request }) => ({ req }),\n        };\n      },\n    }),\n    // SECURITY (CRITICAL-001): RS256 asymmetric verification via the shared\n    // PlatformJwtModule. ai-service is a token CONSUMER, not an issuer.\n    // Replaced the per-service JwtModule.registerAsync block (WS2.B,\n    // 2026-04-14) — single source of truth for all consumer services.\n    PlatformJwtModule,\n    // Event bus for NATS pub/sub\n    EventBusModule.forRootAsync({\n      imports: [ConfigModule],\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n      useFactory: buildEventBusConfig,\n    }),\n    AiOutboxModule,\n    TenantErasureTargetModule.forService('ai-service'),\n    // Redis: Distributed state for rate limiting and token budget counters.\n    // WHY: Without Redis, each ai-service instance maintains its own in-memory\n    // counters, effectively multiplying rate limits by the instance count.\n    RedisModule.forRootAsync({\n      imports: [ConfigModule],\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n      // Build the connection from the platform-standard discrete vars\n      // (REDIS_HOST/PORT/PASSWORD/DB) that compose provides and the cost\n      // services already read — NOT a REDIS_URL that nothing sets, which\n      // silently fell back to redis://localhost:6379 and ECONNREFUSED'd in\n      // every container (the redis service is reachable as host `redis`).\n      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) => {\n        const host = configService.get<string>('REDIS_HOST', 'localhost');\n        const port = configService.get<number>('REDIS_PORT', 6379);\n        const password = configService.get<string>('REDIS_PASSWORD');\n        const db = configService.get<number>('REDIS_DB', 0);\n        const auth = password ? `:${encodeURIComponent(password)}@` : '';\n        return { url: `redis://${auth}${host}:${port}/${db}` };\n      },\n    }),\n    // Rate limiting: applies sliding-window throttling to all GraphQL and REST endpoints.\n    ThrottlerModule,\n    // Tool system\n    ToolRegistryModule,\n    WaterChemistryToolsModule,\n    SensorConfigToolsModule,\n    FarmToolsModule,\n    // FARM-LOW-329: pure aquaculture arithmetic over @platform/aquaculture-engines.\n    AquacultureMathToolsModule,\n    // Feature modules\n    HealthModule,\n    // OBS-HIGH-001: Prometheus GET /metrics scrape endpoint + HTTP metrics\n    // middleware (self-contained platform module — controller is @Public()).\n    ServiceMetricsModule,\n    // ADMIN-HIGH-013: the outbox worker's relay and nightly cleanup route\n    // through the runner's heartbeat (and, for the cleanup, its lease).\n    // ScheduleModule itself arrives with OutboxModule.forFeature.\n    ScheduledJobModule.forRoot({ serviceName: 'ai-service' }),\n    ConversationModule,\n    AgentConfigModule,\n    AuditModule,\n    CostModule,\n    ChatModule,\n    // SENSOR-MEDIUM-070: request.ai.sensor.detectChannels — deterministic\n    // sensor channel detection for backend services (service-principal, no LLM).\n    SensorChannelDetectionModule,\n    // MOB-HIGH-001: human-in-the-loop actuation — proposal persistence +\n    // the request.ai.executeAction responder.\n    ActionsModule,\n    /** SEC-M22: Audit trail infrastructure for compliance tracking. */\n    AuditLogModule.forRoot(),\n    // AUDITTRAIL-CRITICAL-002 sweep — registers AuditedOperationInterceptor.\n    AuditedOperationModule.forRoot(),\n    /**\n     * SECURITY (HIGH-004): Tenant RLS (schema-per-tenant ai).\n     * Conversations and tool executions carry user context — RLS prevents\n     * cross-tenant reads if an app-layer check is bypassed.\n     */\n    RlsModule.forPoolService({\n      serviceName: 'ai',\n      syncTenantSchemas: !aiSchemaDdlOwnedByDbMigrate,\n      excludeTables: getRlsExcludeTablesForService('ai'),\n    }),\n    /**\n     * NEW-H1: Convert TIMESTAMP audit columns to TIMESTAMPTZ at cold start.\n     *\n     * ai-service is schema-per-tenant (mirrors farm/sensor/hr/messaging)\n     * but currently has no TypeORM migration runner — schema concerns\n     * are delivered through SourceSchemaBootstrapService and the related\n     * tenant-sync services. The audit-column bootstrap follows the same\n     * lifecycle and is idempotent.\n     */\n    ...(aiSchemaDdlOwnedByDbMigrate ? [] : [AuditColumnsModule.forRoot({ serviceName: 'ai' })]),\n    /** P11 of 2026-04-14 teardown — runtime schema-drift validator. */\n    // Tenant execution context interceptor (SSoT registration) — keeps the\n    // validated tenant schema in AsyncLocalStorage across Apollo/CQRS async\n    // boundaries so per-tenant search_path routing holds at pg checkout.\n    TenantExecutionContextModule,\n    // Shared tenant schema-existence cache + TenantProvisioned invalidation\n    // (no stale-negative-cache block for freshly provisioned tenants).\n    TenantSchemaCacheModule,\n    SchemaDriftModule.forRoot({ serviceName: 'ai' }),\n  ],\n  providers: [\n    // Migration runner — see const declaration near top of file.\n    AiMigrationRunnerService,\n    // WHY: useFactory bypasses reflect-metadata resolution which fails in Docker Alpine.\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector, configService: ConfigService): TenantGuard =>\n        new TenantGuard(reflector, undefined, configService),\n      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService],\n    },\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector): RolesGuard => new RolesGuard(reflector),\n      inject: [Reflector],\n    },\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (\n        reflector: Reflector,\n        configService: ConfigService,\n        rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy,\n      ): ThrottlerGuard => new ThrottlerGuard(reflector, configService, rateLimiter),\n      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService, SlidingWindowStrategy],\n    },\n    /** SEC-M22: Register global audit logging for compliance — all mutations are tracked. */\n    {\n      provide: APP_INTERCEPTOR,\n      useClass: AuditLogInterceptor,\n    },\n    // Bootstrap source schema tables on startup (creates template tables if missing)\n    SourceSchemaBootstrapService,\n    // Pool-level tenant schema routing (patches pg Pool.connect for search_path injection)\n    TenantConnectionBootstrap,\n    // Auto-sync tenant schemas with source schema (creates missing tables/columns)\n    TenantSchemaSyncService,\n  ],\n})\nexport class AppModule implements NestModule {\n  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer) {\n    // Middleware execution order:\n    // 1. CorrelationIdMiddleware - Add correlation ID for request tracing\n    // 2. UserContextMiddleware - Parse x-user-payload header from gateway\n    // 3. TenantContextMiddleware - Extract tenant from JWT/headers\n    // 4. TenantSchemaMiddleware - Set PostgreSQL search_path to tenant schema\n    // SEC-CRITICAL-002 sweep — strip forged internal headers (safe on all routes\n    // except the health probe).\n    consumer\n      .apply(StripInternalHeadersMiddleware)\n      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n      .forRoutes('*');\n\n    // SEC-HIGH-156: resolve req.user/req.tenantId from the gateway-signed\n    // verified-user assertion (after Strip sets req.verifiedIdentity, before\n    // UserContext). ai-service serves no direct browser REST surface — the\n    // former /api/v2/ai proxy was retired for NATS request.ai.chat — so only\n    // the health probe is excluded (tests/invariants/nginx-route-resolution.spec.ts\n    // keeps nginx and the route table in agreement).\n    consumer\n      .apply(VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware)\n      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n      .forRoutes('*');\n\n    consumer\n      .apply(\n        CorrelationIdMiddleware,\n        RequestContextMiddleware, // Populate AsyncLocalStorage for structured logging\n        UserContextMiddleware,\n        TenantContextMiddleware,\n        TenantSchemaMiddleware,\n      )\n      .exclude('health', 'health/{*path}')\n      .forRoutes('*');\n  }\n}\n","numLines":426,"startLine":1,"totalLines":426}}}

The prior rationales are context to attack, never an answer to copy.\",\n      \"why_it_matters\": \"This verdict is about to be promoted to anchor grade, which suppresses this finding class for test-gap-adapter, quarantines rules, and scores the judges. feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus counts the judges who AGREED with the settled verdict, so a third voice that only restates the pair adds statistical weight without adding information — the anchor would be manufactured rather than earned.\",\n      \"what_breaks_if_skipped\": \"A ratified wrong verdict trains the system in the wrong direction permanently: a wrongly-affirmed true_positive keeps a non-gap in the remediation queue, and a wrongly-affirmed false_positive teaches the adapter to stay silent about a real untested auth exemption. Both are worse than having no third judge, because the error arrives wearing the authority of independent corroboration.\",\n      \"downstream_surface\": \"feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus (verdict + mean_confidence + judge_count), the finding-suppression path for tool_id test-gap-adapter, and judge calibration scoring.\",\n      \"proof_of_result\": \"The file:line reads listed in details.consensus.evidence, each opened in this run.\"\n    },\n    \"independent_reasoning\": {\n      \"leg_1_security_sensitive\": \"tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:271 classifies a file as security-sensitive when its text matches @Public\\\\b | AuthGuard | TenantGuard | PermissionGuard (or when the path names a guard/strategy/auth/security/permission surface). apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.ts:5 carries @Public() and apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.ts:6 carries @SkipTenantGuard(). I deliberately do NOT rest the classification on the second match: TenantGuard matches as an unanchored substring inside SkipTenantGuard, which is regex looseness the rule should not be credited for. @Public() alone carries the leg, and it is a substantive auth-boundary exemption on a federated subgraph query (apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.ts:9), not a naming coincidence.\",\n      \"leg_2_no_coverage_signal\": \"tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:296 accepts four coverage signals: same normalized basename, same-directory prefix match, a sibling __tests__ spec, or a test whose resolved import lands on the source path. I checked each. apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.spec.ts and apps/ai-service/src/health/__tests__/health.resolver.spec.ts are both absent on read. The only importer of the resolver I located is production wiring at apps/ai-service/src/health/health.module.ts:3. The two health specs in the evidence set target different subjects — apps/auth-service/src/health/__tests__/health.controller.spec.ts exercises auth-service's REST HealthController (liveness/readiness/health probes, MFA key checks) and libs/backend-common/src/health/__tests__/standard-health.controller.spec.ts exercises StandardHealthController — and neither imports nor references the ai-service GraphQL resolver. Per tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:168 the finding is emitted only when matchedTests is empty and the path is not allowlisted, and that condition holds.\",\n      \"refutation_attempted_and_rejected\": \"The strongest counter-evidence in the set is e2e/tests/mobile/ai-action-confirm.spec.ts:68, which really does execute `query ProvisionAi { aiServiceHealth }` — the resolver body runs under an e2e test, so a coarse reading could call the file covered. It does not overturn the finding, for three reasons I verified by reading the call site: the query is issued with { token: workerToken }, so the unauthenticated path that @Public() opens is never exercised; the response is discarded rather than asserted, and the call exists to provoke tenant-table provisioning before a seed; and the test reaches the resolver over the wire rather than by import, so it is not an importing signal under the matcher at tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:296. The decisive test of a coverage claim is whether breaking the behavior breaks a test: removing or widening the exemption at apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.ts:5 leaves that e2e assertion green, because a valid token satisfies the guard either way. Reading the counter-evidence strengthened the finding instead of refuting it.\",\n      \"recorded_dissent_not_affecting_verdict\": \"Severity 'high' overstates the exposure. The resolver takes no arguments, touches no data, and returns the constant 'ok' (apps/ai-service/src/health/health.resolver.ts:9), so the blast radius of the untested exemption is the existence of one unauthenticated GraphQL field.
